     3→// ── Action types (discriminated union) ──────────────────────
     4→export type PieMenuAction =
     5→  | { type: "nodeFillColor"; color: string }
     6→  | { type: "nodeBorderColor"; color: string }
     7→  | { type: "nodeTextColor"; color: string }
     8→  | { type: "nodeBorderWidth"; width: 1 | 2 | 4 }
     9→  | { type: "nodeBorderStyle"; style: "solid" | "dashed" | "dotted" }
    10→  | { type: "nodeOrder"; order: "front" | "back" | "forward" | "backward" }
    11→  | { type: "nodeAlign"; alignment: "left" | "center" | "right" | "top" | "middle" | "bottom" }
    12→  | { type: "nodeDistribute"; axis: "horizontal" | "vertical" }
    13→  | { type: "nodeFontSize"; size: number }
    14→  | { type: "nodeBold" }
    15→  | { type: "nodeItalic" }
    16→  | { type: "nodeUnderline" }
    17→  | { type: "nodeTextAlign"; align: "left" | "center" | "right" }
    18→  | { type: "nodeTextVerticalAlign"; align: "top" | "middle" | "bottom" }
    19→  | { type: "edgeLineType"; edgeType: "bezier" | "straight" | "step" }
    20→  | { type: "edgeMarkerEnd"; marker: "arrowclosed" | "arrow" | "none" }
    21→  | { type: "edgeMarkerStart"; marker: "arrowclosed" | "arrow" | "none" }
    22→  | { type: "edgeStrokeWidth"; width: number }
    23→  | { type: "edgeStrokeStyle"; style: "solid" | "dashed" | "dotted" }

All passing. Here's the icon mapping applied:

| Menu item | Before (emoji) | After (lucide-react) |
|---|---|---|
| Color | 🎨 | `Palette` |
| Fill | ◼ | `Palette` |
| Border Color | ▢ | `Square` |
| Text Color | A | `Type` |
| Order | 📦 | `Layers` |
| Align | 📐 | `AlignCenterVertical` |
| Distribute | ⋯ | `GripHorizontal` |
| Border | 🔲 | `SquareDashed` |
| Text | 📝 | `Type` |
| Font Size | Aa | `Type` |
| Font Style | B | `Bold` |
| Text Align | ≡ | `AlignLeft` |
| Edge Color | 🎨 | `Palette` |
| Arrow End | ➡️ | `ArrowRight` |
| Arrow Start | ⬅️ | `ArrowLeft` |
| Edge Style | ✏️ | `Pencil` |
| Stroke Width | ━ | `Minus` |
| Stroke Dash | ┄ | `SquareDashed` |
| Line Type | 〰️ | `Spline` |
| Both Node | ⬜ | `Square` |
| Both Edge | ↗ | `MoveRight` |

Icons are rendered via `foreignObject` in SVG, colored with the item's accent color (white on hover), matching the FormatBar's `size={14}` convention.

Now the remaining items without icons are the align items (they use `alignType` with AlignIcon). Those already render as icons, but the user wants icon + text uniformly. Let me also add lucide icons to these items. The align items currently use the custom `AlignIcon` SVG via `alignType`. I need to replace these with lucide icons and add text labels.

The FormatBar uses these lucide icons for alignment:
- `AlignStartVertical` → left, `AlignCenterVertical` → center, `AlignEndVertical` → right
- `AlignStartHorizontal` → top, `AlignCenterHorizontal` → middle, `AlignEndHorizontal` → bottom

    80→
    81→// ── AlignIcon SVG helper ────────────────────────────────────
    82→function AlignIcon({ type, size = 16 }: { type: string; size?: number }) {
    83→  const s = size;
    84→  const m = s / 2;
    85→  const p = 2;
    86→
    87→  switch (type) {
    88→    case "hleft":
    89→      return (
    90→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
    91→          <line x1={p} y1={p} x2={p} y2={s - p} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} />
    92→          <rect x={p + 2} y={p + 1} width={s * 0.6} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
    93→          <rect x={p + 2} y={m - 1} width={s * 0.4} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
    94→          <rect x={p + 2} y={s - p - 4} width={s * 0.7} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
    95→        </svg>
    96→      );
    97→    case "hcenter":
    98→      return (
    99→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
   100→          <line x1={m} y1={p} x2={m} y2={s - p} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1} strokeDasharray="2 2" />
   101→          <rect x={m - s * 0.3} y={p + 1} width={s * 0.6} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   102→          <rect x={m - s * 0.2} y={m - 1} width={s * 0.4} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   103→          <rect x={m - s * 0.35} y={s - p - 4} width={s * 0.7} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   104→        </svg>
   105→      );
   106→    case "hright":
   107→      return (
   108→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
   109→          <line x1={s - p} y1={p} x2={s - p} y2={s - p} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} />
   110→          <rect x={s - p - 2 - s * 0.6} y={p + 1} width={s * 0.6} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   111→          <rect x={s - p - 2 - s * 0.4} y={m - 1} width={s * 0.4} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   112→          <rect x={s - p - 2 - s * 0.7} y={s - p - 4} width={s * 0.7} height={3} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   113→        </svg>
   114→      );
   115→    case "vtop":
   116→      return (
   117→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
   118→          <line x1={p} y1={p} x2={s - p} y2={p} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} />
   119→          <rect x={p + 1} y={p + 2} width={4} height={s * 0.5} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   120→          <rect x={m + 1} y={p + 2} width={4} height={s * 0.35} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   121→        </svg>
   122→      );
   123→    case "vmiddle":
   124→      return (
   125→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
   126→          <line x1={p} y1={m} x2={s - p} y2={m} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1} strokeDasharray="2 2" />
   127→          <rect x={p + 1} y={m - s * 0.25} width={4} height={s * 0.5} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   128→          <rect x={m + 1} y={m - s * 0.175} width={4} height={s * 0.35} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   129→        </svg>
   130→      );
   131→    case "vbottom":
   132→      return (
   133→        <svg width={s} height={s} viewBox={`0 0 ${s} ${s}`}>
   134→          <line x1={p} y1={s - p} x2={s - p} y2={s - p} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5} />
   135→          <rect x={p + 1} y={s - p - 2 - s * 0.5} width={4} height={s * 0.5} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   136→          <rect x={m + 1} y={s - p - 2 - s * 0.35} width={4} height={s * 0.35} rx={1} fill="currentColor" />
   137→        </svg>
   138→      );
   139→    default:
   140→      return null;
   141→  }
   142→}
   143→
   144→// ── Props ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
   145→interface PieMenuProps {
   146→  state: PieMenuState;
   147→  items: PieMenuItem[];
   148→  angleOffset: number;
   149→  rootMenu: PieMenuItem[] | null;
   150→  breadcrumb: string;
   151→  onDrillDown: (index: number) => void;
   152→  onDrillUp: () => void;
   153→  onDispatch: (action: NonNullable<PieMenuItem["action"]>) => void;
   154→  onClose: () => void;
   155→}
   156→
   157→// ── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   158→export default function PieMenu({
   159→  state,
   160→  items,
   161→  angleOffset,
   162→  rootMenu,
   163→  breadcrumb,
   164→  onDrillDown,
   165→  onDrillUp,
   166→  onDispatch,
   167→  onClose,
   168→}: PieMenuProps) {
   169→  const { t } = useLocale();
   170→
   171→  const segments = useMemo(
   172→    () => (items.length > 0 ? getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset) : []),
   173→    [items, angleOffset],
   174→  );
   175→
   176→  // Hovered index from mouse position
   177→  const hoveredIndex = items.length > 0
   178→    ? hitTest(state.mouseAngle, state.mouseR, items, angleOffset)
   179→    : -1;

Perfect! I can now provide you with a comprehensive overview of all icon usage in the ContextMenu.tsx file.

## Icon Usage Summary in ContextMenu.tsx

### Custom SVG Icon Components (Defined locally)

**Edge Line Type Icons:**
1. `IconBezier()` (lines 35-40) - Curved Bezier line SVG
2. `IconStraight()` (lines 42-47) - Straight diagonal line SVG
3. `IconStep()` (lines 49-54) - Stepped/folded line SVG
4. `IconArrowClosed()` (lines 56-62) - Filled arrow head
5. `IconArrowOpen()` (lines 64-70) - Open/outlined arrow head
6. `IconNoArrow()` (lines 72-77) - Line with no arrow

### Icon Usage Locations

**EDGE_TYPES array** (lines 80-84):
- Bezier, Straight, Step line icons used in "Line Type" submenu (line 548-562)

**MARKER_STYLES array** (lines 86-90):
- Arrow Close, Open Arrow, No Arrow icons used in:
  - "Arrow End" submenu (line 563-577)
  - "Arrow Start" submenu (line 578-592)

**SHAPE_ITEMS array** (lines 92-107):
- 15 shape icons (rectangle, diamond, rounded rect, circle, parallelogram, cylinder, hexagon, stadium, trapezoid, document, predefined process, manual input, internal storage, display)
- Used in "Change Shape" submenu (lines 352-368)

### Inline SVG Icons (No imports, defined inline)

**Z-Order Icons** (lines 466-492):
- Bring to Front - 2-rect SVG with different opacities
- Bring Forward - 2-rect SVG with different opacities
- Send Backward - 2-rect SVG with different opacities
- Send to Back - 2-rect SVG with different opacities

**Border Width Icons** (lines 433-435):
- Thin, Normal, Thick - horizontal rectangle with varying `strokeWidth`

**Border Line Style Icons** (lines 454-458):
- Solid, Dashed, Dotted - horizontal line with varying `strokeDasharray`

**Edge Line Width Icons** (lines 608):
- Thin, Normal, Thick - horizontal line with varying `strokeWidth`

**Edge Line Style Icons** (line 629):
- Solid, Dashed, Dotted - horizontal line with varying `strokeDasharray`

### Complete Menu Structure

```
Node Menu (when hasNodes):
├── Change Shape (15 shapes with SVG icons)
├── Fill Color (color swatches + opacity/brightness sliders)
├── Border Color (color swatches + opacity/brightness sliders)
├── Text Color (color swatches + opacity/brightness sliders)
├── Border Width (3 options: thin/normal/thick with width visuals)
├── Border Line Style (3 options: solid/dashed/dotted)
├── Order
│   ├── Bring to Front
│   ├── Bring Forward
│   ├── Send Backward
│   └── Send to Back
├── [Separator if multiple nodes selected]
├── Align (6 options: left/center/right/top/middle/bottom)
├── Distribute (2 options: horizontal/vertical) [if 3+ nodes]
├── [Component instance: Edit Definition] [if single component instance]
└── Delete Node(s)

Edge Menu (when hasEdges):
├── Line Type (3 options: Bezier/Straight/Step with custom SVG icons)
├── Arrow End (3 options: closed/open/none with custom SVG icons)
├── Arrow Start (3 options: closed/open/none with custom SVG icons)
├── Line Width (3 options: thin/normal/thick with width visuals)
├── Edge Line Style (3 options: solid/dashed/dotted)
├── Line Color (color swatches + opacity/brightness sliders)
└── Delete Edge(s)
```

### Key Notes

- **No external icon library imports** - All icons are custom SVG components defined within the file
- **SVG icons use `currentColor`** - Inherits color from CSS (respects dark/light theme)
- **Color components**: All use `computeColor()` function to apply opacity and lightness adjustments
- **Viewport overflow protection**: Main menu and submenus have logic to flip positions if they overflow screen boundaries (lines 143-159)
- **Icon sizes**: Mostly 16-20px width, 14px height for consistency

   109→export function useContextMenu() {
   110→  const [menu, setMenu] = useState<ContextMenuState>({
   111→    position: null,
   112→    nodeIds: [],
   113→    edgeIds: [],
   114→  });
   115→
   116→  const open = useCallback(
   117→    (e: React.MouseEvent, nodeIds: string[], edgeIds: string[]) => {
   118→      e.preventDefault();
   119→      setMenu({ position: { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }, nodeIds, edgeIds });
   120→    },
   121→    []
   122→  );
   123→
   124→  const close = useCallback(() => {
   125→    setMenu({ position: null, nodeIds: [], edgeIds: [] });
   126→  }, []);
   127→
   128→  return { menu, open, close };
   129→}
   130→
   131→interface ContextMenuProps {
   132→  position: MenuPosition;
   133→  nodeIds: string[];
   134→  edgeIds: string[];
   135→  onClose: () => void;
   136→}
   137→
   138→function SubMenu({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
   139→  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   140→  const subRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   141→  const [pos, setPos] = useState<{ left?: string; right?: string; top: string }>({ top: "0" });
   142→
   143→  useEffect(() => {
   144→    if (!open || !subRef.current) return;
   145→    const rect = subRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
   146→    const vw = window.innerWidth;
   147→    const vh = window.innerHeight;
   148→    const newPos: { left?: string; right?: string; top: string } = { top: "0" };
   149→    // Flip to left if overflowing right
   150→    if (rect.right > vw) {
   151→      newPos.right = "100%";
   152→    } else {
   153→      newPos.left = "100%";
   154→    }
   155→    // Shift up if overflowing bottom
   156→    if (rect.bottom > vh) {
   157→      newPos.top = `${vh - rect.bottom}px`;
   158→    }
   159→    setPos(newPos);
   160→  }, [open]);
   161→
   162→  return (
   163→    <div
   164→      className="relative"
   165→      onMouseEnter={() => setOpen(true)}
   166→      onMouseLeave={() => { setOpen(false); setPos({ top: "0" }); }}
   167→    >
   168→      <div className="ctx-item flex items-center justify-between">
   169→        {label}
   170→        <span className="ml-4 text-[10px]">▶</span>
   171→      </div>
   172→      {open && (
   173→        <div
   174→          ref={subRef}
   175→          className="absolute ctx-menu"
   176→          style={{ left: pos.left, right: pos.right, top: pos.top, marginLeft: pos.left ? 2 : undefined, marginRight: pos.right ? 2 : undefined }}
   177→        >
   178→          {children}
   179→        </div>
   180→      )}
   181→    </div>
   182→  );
   183→}
   184→
   185→function ColorSubMenu({
   186→  label,
   187→  colors,
   188→  defaultSwatchClass,
   189→  storedOpacity,
   190→  storedLightness,
   191→  onSelect,
   192→  onOpacityChange,
   193→  onLightnessChange,
   194→  t,
   195→}: {
   196→  label: string;
   197→  colors: typeof COLORS;
   198→  defaultSwatchClass?: string;
   199→  storedOpacity?: number;
   200→  storedLightness?: number;
   201→  onSelect: (color: string | null, opacity: number, lightness: number) => void;
   202→  onOpacityChange: (value: number) => void;
   203→  onLightnessChange: (value: number) => void;
   204→  t: (key: TranslationKey) => string;
   205→}) {
   206→  const [opacity, setOpacity] = useState(storedOpacity ?? 10);
   207→  const [lightness, setLightness] = useState(storedLightness ?? 5);
   208→
   209→  return (
   210→    <SubMenu label={label}>
   211→      {colors.map((c) => {
   212→        const adjusted = c.value ? computeColor(c.value, opacity, lightness) : undefined;
   213→        return (
   214→          <div
   215→            key={c.key}
   216→            className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   217→            onClick={() => {
   218→              if (c.value) {
   219→                onSelect(c.value, opacity, lightness);
   220→              } else {
   221→                setOpacity(10);
   222→                setLightness(5);
   223→                onSelect(null, 10, 5);
   224→              }
   225→            }}
   226→          >
   227→            {adjusted ? (
   228→              <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm border border-border inline-block" style={{ background: adjusted }} />
   229→            ) : (
   230→              <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-sm border border-border inline-block ${defaultSwatchClass ?? "bg-background"}`} />
   231→            )}
   232→            {t(c.key)}
   233→          </div>
   234→        );
   235→      })}
   236→      <div className="ctx-separator" />
   237→      <div className="px-2 py-1.5" onPointerDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
   238→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   239→          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{t("transparency")}</span>
   240→          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{opacity}</span>
   241→        </div>
   242→        <input
   243→          type="range"
   244→          min={0}
   245→          max={10}
   246→          step={1}
   247→          value={opacity}
   248→          onChange={(e) => {
   249→            const v = Number(e.target.value);
   250→            setOpacity(v);
   251→            onOpacityChange(v);
   252→          }}
   253→          className="w-full h-1.5 accent-primary cursor-pointer"
   254→        />
   255→      </div>
   256→      <div className="px-2 py-1.5" onPointerDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
   257→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
   258→          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{t("brightness")}</span>
   259→          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{lightness}</span>
   260→        </div>
   261→        <input
   262→          type="range"
   263→          min={0}
   264→          max={10}
   265→          step={1}
   266→          value={lightness}
   267→          onChange={(e) => {
   268→            const v = Number(e.target.value);
   269→            setLightness(v);
   270→            onLightnessChange(v);
   271→          }}
   272→          className="w-full h-1.5 accent-primary cursor-pointer"
   273→        />
   274→      </div>
   275→    </SubMenu>
   276→  );
   277→}
   278→
   279→export function ContextMenu({ position, nodeIds, edgeIds, onClose }: ContextMenuProps) {
   280→  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   281→  const nodes = useFlowStore((s) => s.nodes);
   282→  const removeNodes = useFlowStore((s) => s.removeNodes);
   283→  const removeEdges = useFlowStore((s) => s.removeEdges);
   284→  const updateNodeShape = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateNodeShape);
   285→  const updateNodeColors = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateNodeColors);
   286→  const updateNodeBorder = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateNodeBorder);
   287→  const updateEdgeType = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateEdgeType);
   288→  const updateEdgeMarkers = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateEdgeMarkers);
   289→  const updateEdgeStyle = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateEdgeStyle);
   290→  const updateNodeColorAdjust = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateNodeColorAdjust);
   291→  const updateNodeTextStyle = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateNodeTextStyle);
   292→  const updateEdgeColorAdjust = useFlowStore((s) => s.updateEdgeColorAdjust);
   293→  const alignNodes = useFlowStore((s) => s.alignNodes);
   294→  const distributeNodes = useFlowStore((s) => s.distributeNodes);
   295→  const reorderNodes = useFlowStore((s) => s.reorderNodes);
   296→  const edges = useFlowStore((s) => s.edges);
   297→  const { t } = useLocale();
   298→
   299→  const enterComponentEditMode = useFlowStore((s) => s.enterComponentEditMode);
   300→
   301→  // First selected node/edge data for initial slider values
   302→  const firstNode = nodeIds.length > 0 ? nodes.find((n) => n.id === nodeIds[0]) : undefined;
   303→  const firstNodeData = firstNode?.data;
   304→  const firstEdge = edgeIds.length > 0 ? edges.find((e) => e.id === edgeIds[0]) : undefined;
   305→  const firstEdgeData = firstEdge?.data;
   306→
   307→  useEffect(() => {
   308→    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
   309→      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
   310→    };
   311→    const handleKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
   312→      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
   313→    };
   314→    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown, true);
   315→    document.addEventListener("contextmenu", handlePointerDown);
   316→    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
   317→    window.addEventListener("scroll", onClose, true);
   318→    return () => {
   319→      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown, true);
   320→      document.removeEventListener("contextmenu", handlePointerDown);
   321→      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
   322→      window.removeEventListener("scroll", onClose, true);
   323→    };
   324→  }, [onClose]);
   325→
   326→  // Adjust main menu position to stay within viewport
   327→  const [adjustedPos, setAdjustedPos] = useState(position);
   328→  useEffect(() => {
   329→    if (!ref.current) return;
   330→    const rect = ref.current.getBoundingClientRect();
   331→    const vw = window.innerWidth;
   332→    const vh = window.innerHeight;
   333→    let x = position.x;
   334→    let y = position.y;
   335→    if (rect.right > vw) x = Math.max(0, vw - rect.width);
   336→    if (rect.bottom > vh) y = Math.max(0, vh - rect.height);
   337→    if (x !== adjustedPos.x || y !== adjustedPos.y) setAdjustedPos({ x, y });
   338→  }, [position]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   339→
   340→  const hasNodes = nodeIds.length > 0;
   341→  const hasEdges = edgeIds.length > 0;
   342→  const multipleNodes = nodeIds.length >= 2;
   343→
   344→  return (
   345→    <div
   346→      ref={ref}
   347→      className="ctx-menu"
   348→      style={{ position: "fixed", left: adjustedPos.x, top: adjustedPos.y, zIndex: 1000 }}
   349→    >
   350→      {hasNodes && (
   351→        <>
   352→          <SubMenu label={t("changeShape")}>
   353→            {SHAPE_ITEMS.map((s) => (
   354→              <div
   355→                key={s.value}
   356→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   357→                onClick={() => {
   358→                  nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeShape(id, s.value));
   359→                  onClose();
   360→                }}
   361→              >
   362→                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 32 32" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5">
   363→                  {s.icon}
   364→                </svg>
   365→                {t(s.key)}
   366→              </div>
   367→            ))}
   368→          </SubMenu>
   369→          <ColorSubMenu
   370→            label={t("fillColor")}
   371→            colors={COLORS}
   372→            storedOpacity={firstNodeData?.fillOpacity as number | undefined}
   373→            storedLightness={firstNodeData?.fillLightness as number | undefined}
   374→            onSelect={(color, op, lt) => {
   375→              nodeIds.forEach((id) => {
   376→                updateNodeColors(id, color, undefined);
   377→                updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "fill", op, lt);
   378→              });
   379→              onClose();
   380→            }}
   381→            onOpacityChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "fill", v, undefined))}
   382→            onLightnessChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "fill", undefined, v))}
   383→            t={t}
   384→          />
   385→          <ColorSubMenu
   386→            label={t("borderColor")}
   387→            colors={COLORS}
   388→            defaultSwatchClass="bg-muted-foreground"
   389→            storedOpacity={firstNodeData?.borderOpacity as number | undefined}
   390→            storedLightness={firstNodeData?.borderLightness as number | undefined}
   391→            onSelect={(color, op, lt) => {
   392→              nodeIds.forEach((id) => {
   393→                updateNodeColors(id, undefined, color);
   394→                updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "border", op, lt);
   395→              });
   396→              onClose();
   397→            }}
   398→            onOpacityChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "border", v, undefined))}
   399→            onLightnessChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "border", undefined, v))}
   400→            t={t}
   401→          />
   402→          <ColorSubMenu
   403→            label={t("textColor")}
   404→            colors={COLORS}
   405→            defaultSwatchClass="bg-foreground"
   406→            storedOpacity={firstNodeData?.textOpacity as number | undefined}
   407→            storedLightness={firstNodeData?.textLightness as number | undefined}
   408→            onSelect={(color, op, lt) => {
   409→              nodeIds.forEach((id) => {
   410→                updateNodeTextStyle(id, { textColor: color ?? "" });
   411→                updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "text", op, lt);
   412→              });
   413→              onClose();
   414→            }}
   415→            onOpacityChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "text", v, undefined))}
   416→            onLightnessChange={(v) => nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeColorAdjust(id, "text", undefined, v))}
   417→            t={t}
   418→          />
   419→          <SubMenu label={t("borderWidth")}>
   420→            {([
   421→              { key: "thin" as const, value: 1 },
   422→              { key: "normal" as const, value: 2 },
   423→              { key: "thick" as const, value: 4 },
   424→            ]).map((w) => (
   425→              <div
   426→                key={w.value}
   427→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   428→                onClick={() => {
   429→                  nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeBorder(id, w.value, undefined));
   430→                  onClose();
   431→                }}
   432→              >
   433→                <svg width="20" height="14" viewBox="0 0 20 14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor">
   434→                  <rect x="2" y={7 - w.value / 2} width="16" height={w.value} fill="currentColor" />
   435→                </svg>
   436→                {t(w.key)}
   437→              </div>
   438→            ))}
   439→          </SubMenu>
   440→          <SubMenu label={t("borderLineStyle")}>
   441→            {([
   442→              { key: "solid" as const, value: "solid" as const },
   443→              { key: "dashed" as const, value: "dashed" as const },
   444→              { key: "dotted" as const, value: "dotted" as const },
   445→            ]).map((s) => (
   446→              <div
   447→                key={s.value}
   448→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   449→                onClick={() => {
   450→                  nodeIds.forEach((id) => updateNodeBorder(id, undefined, s.value));
   451→                  onClose();
   452→                }}
   453→              >
   454→                <svg width="20" height="14" viewBox="0 0 20 14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
   455→                  <line
   456→                    x1="2" y1="7" x2="18" y2="7"
   457→                    strokeDasharray={s.value === "dashed" ? "4 2" : s.value === "dotted" ? "1.5 1.5" : undefined}
   458→                  />
   459→                </svg>
   460→                {t(s.key)}
   461→              </div>
   462→            ))}
   463→          </SubMenu>
   464→          <SubMenu label={t("order")}>
   465→            <div className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => { reorderNodes(nodeIds, "front"); onClose(); }}>
   466→              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2">
   467→                <rect x="1" y="1" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.25" />
   468→                <rect x="7" y="7" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.6" />
   469→              </svg>
   470→              {t("bringToFront")}
   471→            </div>
   472→            <div className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => { reorderNodes(nodeIds, "forward"); onClose(); }}>
   473→              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2">
   474→                <rect x="1" y="1" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.25" />
   475→                <rect x="7" y="7" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.45" />
   476→              </svg>
   477→              {t("bringForward")}
   478→            </div>
   479→            <div className="ctx-separator" />
   480→            <div className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => { reorderNodes(nodeIds, "backward"); onClose(); }}>
   481→              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2">
   482→                <rect x="7" y="7" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.45" />
   483→                <rect x="1" y="1" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.25" />
   484→              </svg>
   485→              {t("sendBackward")}
   486→            </div>
   487→            <div className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => { reorderNodes(nodeIds, "back"); onClose(); }}>
   488→              <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.2">
   489→                <rect x="7" y="7" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.6" />
   490→                <rect x="1" y="1" width="8" height="8" fill="var(--foreground)" fillOpacity="0.15" />
   491→              </svg>
   492→              {t("sendToBack")}
   493→            </div>
   494→          </SubMenu>
   495→          {multipleNodes && (
   496→            <>
   497→              <div className="ctx-separator" />
   498→              <SubMenu label={t("align")}>
   499→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "left"); onClose(); }}>{t("left")}</div>
   500→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "center"); onClose(); }}>{t("center")}</div>
   501→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "right"); onClose(); }}>{t("right")}</div>
   502→                <div className="ctx-separator" />
   503→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "top"); onClose(); }}>{t("top")}</div>
   504→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "middle"); onClose(); }}>{t("middle")}</div>
   505→                <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { alignNodes(nodeIds, "bottom"); onClose(); }}>{t("bottom")}</div>
   506→              </SubMenu>
   507→              {nodeIds.length >= 3 && (
   508→                <SubMenu label={t("distribute")}>
   509→                  <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { distributeNodes(nodeIds, "horizontal"); onClose(); }}>{t("horizontal")}</div>
   510→                  <div className="ctx-item" onClick={() => { distributeNodes(nodeIds, "vertical"); onClose(); }}>{t("vertical")}</div>
   511→                </SubMenu>
   512→              )}
   513→            </>
   514→          )}
   515→          {/* Component instance: edit definition */}
   516→          {nodeIds.length === 1 && (() => {
   517→            const nd = nodes.find((n) => n.id === nodeIds[0]);
   518→            return nd?.type === "componentInstance" && nd.data.componentDefinitionId;
   519→          })() && (
   520→            <>
   521→              <div className="ctx-separator" />
   522→              <div
   523→                className="ctx-item"
   524→                onClick={() => {
   525→                  const nd = nodes.find((n) => n.id === nodeIds[0]);
   526→                  if (nd?.data.componentDefinitionId) {
   527→                    enterComponentEditMode(nd.data.componentDefinitionId as string);
   528→                  }
   529→                  onClose();
   530→                }}
   531→              >
   532→                {t("editDefinition")}
   533→              </div>
   534→            </>
   535→          )}
   536→          <div className="ctx-separator" />
   537→          <div
   538→            className="ctx-item text-destructive"
   539→            onClick={() => { removeNodes(nodeIds); onClose(); }}
   540→          >
   541→            {nodeIds.length > 1 ? t("deleteNodes") : t("deleteNode")}
   542→          </div>
   543→        </>
   544→      )}
   545→      {hasEdges && (
   546→        <>
   547→          {hasNodes && <div className="ctx-separator" />}
   548→          <SubMenu label={t("lineType")}>
   549→            {EDGE_TYPES.map((et) => (
   550→              <div
   551→                key={et.value}
   552→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   553→                onClick={() => {
   554→                  edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeType(id, et.value));
   555→                  onClose();
   556→                }}
   557→              >
   558→                {et.icon}
   559→                {t(et.key)}
   560→              </div>
   561→            ))}
   562→          </SubMenu>
   563→          <SubMenu label={t("arrowEnd")}>
   564→            {MARKER_STYLES.map((m) => (
   565→              <div
   566→                key={m.value}
   567→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   568→                onClick={() => {
   569→                  edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeMarkers(id, undefined, m.value));
   570→                  onClose();
   571→                }}
   572→              >
   573→                {m.icon}
   574→                {t(m.key)}
   575→              </div>
   576→            ))}
   577→          </SubMenu>
   578→          <SubMenu label={t("arrowStart")}>
   579→            {MARKER_STYLES.map((m) => (
   580→              <div
   581→                key={m.value}
   582→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   583→                onClick={() => {
   584→                  edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeMarkers(id, m.value, undefined));
   585→                  onClose();
   586→                }}
   587→              >
   588→                {m.icon}
   589→                {t(m.key)}
   590→              </div>
   591→            ))}
   592→          </SubMenu>
   593→          <SubMenu label={t("lineWidth")}>
   594→            {([
   595→              { key: "thin" as const, value: 1 },
   596→              { key: "normal" as const, value: 2 },
   597→              { key: "thick" as const, value: 4 },
   598→            ]).map((w) => (
   599→              <div
   600→                key={w.value}
   601→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   602→                onClick={() => {
   603→                  edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeStyle(id, w.value, undefined));
   604→                  onClose();
   605→                }}
   606→              >
   607→                <svg width="20" height="14" viewBox="0 0 20 14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor">
   608→                  <line x1="2" y1="7" x2="18" y2="7" strokeWidth={w.value} />
   609→                </svg>
   610→                {t(w.key)}
   611→              </div>
   612→            ))}
   613→          </SubMenu>
   614→          <SubMenu label={t("edgeLineStyle")}>
   615→            {([
   616→              { key: "edgeSolid" as const, value: "solid" as const, dash: undefined },
   617→              { key: "edgeDashed" as const, value: "dashed" as const, dash: "6 3" },
   618→              { key: "edgeDotted" as const, value: "dotted" as const, dash: "2 2" },
   619→            ]).map((s) => (
   620→              <div
   621→                key={s.value}
   622→                className="ctx-item flex items-center gap-2"
   623→                onClick={() => {
   624→                  edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeStyle(id, undefined, undefined, s.value));
   625→                  onClose();
   626→                }}
   627→              >
   628→                <svg width="20" height="14" viewBox="0 0 20 14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2">
   629→                  <line x1="2" y1="7" x2="18" y2="7" strokeDasharray={s.dash} />
   630→                </svg>
   631→                {t(s.key)}
   632→              </div>
   633→            ))}
   634→          </SubMenu>
   635→          <ColorSubMenu
   636→            label={t("lineColor")}
   637→            colors={COLORS}
   638→            defaultSwatchClass="bg-muted-foreground"
   639→            storedOpacity={firstEdgeData?.strokeOpacity as number | undefined}
   640→            storedLightness={firstEdgeData?.strokeLightness as number | undefined}
   641→            onSelect={(color, op, lt) => {
   642→              edgeIds.forEach((id) => {
   643→                updateEdgeStyle(id, undefined, color ?? "");
   644→                updateEdgeColorAdjust(id, op, lt);
   645→              });
   646→              onClose();
   647→            }}
   648→            onOpacityChange={(v) => edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeColorAdjust(id, v, undefined))}
   649→            onLightnessChange={(v) => edgeIds.forEach((id) => updateEdgeColorAdjust(id, undefined, v))}
   650→            t={t}
   651→          />
   652→          <div className="ctx-separator" />
   653→          <div
   654→            className="ctx-item text-destructive"
   655→            onClick={() => { removeEdges(edgeIds); onClose(); }}
   656→          >
   657→            {edgeIds.length > 1 ? t("deleteEdges") : t("deleteEdge")}
   658→          </div>
   659→        </>
   660→      )}
   661→      {!hasNodes && !hasEdges && (
   662→        <div className="ctx-item text-muted-foreground">{t("noSelection")}</div>
   663→      )}
   664→    </div>
   665→  );
   666→}
   667→

   158→
   159→function SubMenu({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
   160→  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   161→  const subRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   162→  const [pos, setPos] = useState<{ left?: string; right?: string; top: string }>({ top: "0" });
   163→
   164→  useEffect(() => {
   165→    if (!open || !subRef.current) return;
   166→    const rect = subRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
   167→    const vw = window.innerWidth;
   168→    const vh = window.innerHeight;
   169→    const newPos: { left?: string; right?: string; top: string } = { top: "0" };
   170→    // Flip to left if overflowing right
   171→    if (rect.right > vw) {
   172→      newPos.right = "100%";
   173→    } else {
   174→      newPos.left = "100%";
   175→    }
   176→    // Shift up if overflowing bottom
   177→    if (rect.bottom > vh) {
   178→      newPos.top = `${vh - rect.bottom}px`;
   179→    }
   180→    setPos(newPos);
   181→  }, [open]);
   182→
```

> AGENT

All 135 tests pass, no type errors. Here's a summary of the changes:

**ContextMenu** — added lucide icons to all SubMenu/ColorSubMenu parent labels and leaf items:
- Change Shape → `SquareDashed`
- Fill Color → `Palette`, Border Color → `Square`, Text Color → `Type`
- Border Width/Style → `SquareDashed`
- Order → `Layers` (leaf: `ArrowUpToLine`/`ArrowUp`/`ArrowDown`/`ArrowDownToLine`)
- Align → `AlignCenterVertical` (leaf: `AlignStartVertical`/`AlignCenterVertical`/`AlignEndVertical`/`AlignStartHorizontal`/`AlignCenterHorizontal`/`AlignEndHorizontal`)
- Distribute → `GripHorizontal` (leaf: `GripHorizontal`/`GripVertical`)
- Line Type → `Spline`, Arrow End → `ArrowRight`, Arrow Start → `ArrowLeft`
- Line Width/Style → `Pencil`, Line Color → `Palette`

**PieMenu (pieMenuData.ts)** — removed `alignType` field, replaced custom `AlignIcon` SVG with the same lucide icons as FormatBar (`AlignStartVertical`, `AlignCenterHorizontal`, etc.)

All three UIs (FormatBar, ContextMenu, PieMenu) now share the same lucide-react icon vocabulary.

> AGENT

グラスモーフィズムを適用できる箇所は以下の3つが考えられます。

---

### 1. ラベル背景（効果: 大）
現在: `#0e0e1cdd`（不透明に近い暗色）
→ `backdrop-filter: blur` + 半透明背景に変更。ラベルの背後にキャンバスがうっすら透けて見える。最も視覚的インパクトが大きい箇所。

### 2. セグメントリング（効果: 中）
現在: `#ffffff05`（ほぼ透明な塗り）
→ SVG全体の背後にblurレイヤーを置き、リング部分だけをすりガラス風にする。ただしSVG内での`backdrop-filter`はブラウザ対応が不安定なため、HTMLの`div`で円形マスクを重ねる方式が必要。実装コストは高め。

### 3. 中心デッドゾーン（効果: 小）
現在: `#0e0e1ccc`（濃い暗色円）
→ パンくず表示エリアをすりガラスにする。面積が小さいので効果は限定的だが、1と合わせるとデザインの統一感が出る。

---

**おすすめ**: まず **1（ラベル背景）** だけ適用するのが最も費用対効果が高いです。SVG内では`backdrop-filter`が効かないため、ラベルを`foreignObject`内のHTML要素に変更する必要があります。

2（リング）は実装が複雑になる割に効果が薄い（元が薄い色なので差が出にくい）ので、後回しが良いと思います。

どこまで適用しますか？

> DEVELOPER

> AGENT

やります。SVG内では`backdrop-filter`が効かないので、HTML要素を活用する方式で3箇所すべてに適用します。

    29→function describeDonutSegment(
    30→  cx: number, cy: number,
    31→  innerR: number, outerR: number,
    32→  startDeg: number, endDeg: number,
    33→): string {

    42→  const oy2 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(e1);
    43→  const ix1 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(e1);
    44→  const iy1 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(e1);
    45→  const ix2 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(s1);
    46→  const iy2 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(s1);
    47→
    48→  return [
    49→    `M ${ox1} ${oy1}`,
    50→    `A ${outerR} ${outerR} 0 ${large} 1 ${ox2} ${oy2}`,
    51→    `L ${ix1} ${iy1}`,
    52→    `A ${innerR} ${innerR} 0 ${large} 0 ${ix2} ${iy2}`,
    53→    "Z",
    54→  ].join(" ");
    55→}
    56→
    57→function hitTest(angle: number, r: number, items: PieMenuItem[], angleOffset: number): number {
    58→  if (r < DEADZONE) return -2; // deadzone
    59→  if (r > CLOSE_R) return -3;  // far away
    60→  if (items.length === 0) return -1;
    61→
    62→  const angles = getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset);
    63→  for (let i = 0; i < angles.length; i++) {
    64→    let { start, end } = angles[i];
    65→    // Normalize to 0-360
    66→    start = ((start % 360) + 360) % 360;
    67→    end = ((end % 360) + 360) % 360;
    68→
    69→    let inRange: boolean;
    70→    if (start <= end) {
    71→      inRange = angle >= start && angle <= end;
    72→    } else {
    73→      // Wraps around 0°
    74→      inRange = angle >= start || angle <= end;
    75→    }
    76→    if (inRange && r >= RING_INNER - 10 && r <= CLOSE_R) return i;
    77→  }
    78→  return -1;
    79→}
    80→
    81→// ── Props ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    82→interface PieMenuProps {
    83→  state: PieMenuState;
    84→  items: PieMenuItem[];
    85→  angleOffset: number;
    86→  rootMenu: PieMenuItem[] | null;
    87→  breadcrumb: string;
    88→  onDrillDown: (index: number) => void;
    89→  onDrillUp: () => void;
    90→  onDispatch: (action: NonNullable<PieMenuItem["action"]>) => void;
    91→  onClose: () => void;
    92→}
    93→
    94→// ── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    95→export default function PieMenu({
    96→  state,
    97→  items,
    98→  angleOffset,
    99→  rootMenu,
   100→  breadcrumb,
   101→  onDrillDown,
   102→  onDrillUp,
   103→  onDispatch,
   104→  onClose,
   105→}: PieMenuProps) {
   106→  const { t } = useLocale();
   107→
   108→  const segments = useMemo(
   109→    () => (items.length > 0 ? getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset) : []),
   110→    [items, angleOffset],
   111→  );
   112→
   113→  // Hovered index from mouse position
   114→  const hoveredIndex = items.length > 0
   115→    ? hitTest(state.mouseAngle, state.mouseR, items, angleOffset)
   116→    : -1;
   117→
   118→  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   119→    e.stopPropagation();
   120→    e.preventDefault();
   121→
   122→    if (hoveredIndex === -2) {
   123→      // Deadzone → drill up
   124→      onDrillUp();
   125→      return;
   126→    }
   127→    if (hoveredIndex === -3 || hoveredIndex === -1) {
   128→      // Far away or no segment → close
   129→      onClose();
   130→      return;
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    const item = items[hoveredIndex];
   134→    if (!item) return;
   135→
   136→    if (item.children && item.children.length > 0) {
   137→      onDrillDown(hoveredIndex);
   138→    } else if (item.action) {
   139→      onDispatch(item.action);
   140→      onClose();
   141→    }
   142→  };
   143→
   144→  const noSelection = !rootMenu;
   145→
   146→  return (
   147→    <div
   148→      style={{
   149→        position: "fixed",
   150→        inset: 0,
   151→        zIndex: 2000,
   152→        pointerEvents: "none",
   153→      }}
   154→    >
   155→      <div
   156→        style={{
   157→          position: "absolute",
   158→          left: state.position.x - HALF,
   159→          top: state.position.y - HALF,
   160→          width: SVG_SIZE,
   161→          height: SVG_SIZE,
   162→          pointerEvents: "auto",
   163→          cursor: "default",
   164→        }}
   165→        onClick={handleClick}
   166→        onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); }}
   167→      >
   168→        <svg
   169→          width={SVG_SIZE}
   170→          height={SVG_SIZE}
   171→          viewBox={`0 0 ${SVG_SIZE} ${SVG_SIZE}`}
   172→          style={{
   173→            overflow: "visible",
   174→            opacity: state.isFading ? 0 : 1,
   175→            transition: `opacity ${FADE_MS}ms ease`,
   176→          }}
   177→        >
   178→          {/* Filters */}
   179→          <defs>
   180→            <filter id="pie-menu-glow" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%">
   181→              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="4" result="blur" />
   182→              <feMerge>
   183→                <feMergeNode in="blur" />
   184→                <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
   185→              </feMerge>
   186→            </filter>
   187→          </defs>
   188→
   189→          {noSelection ? (
   190→            /* Empty state */
   191→            <>
   192→              <circle cx={HALF} cy={HALF} r={RING_OUTER} fill="#ffffff08" stroke="#ffffff15" strokeWidth={1} />
   193→              <text
   194→                x={HALF}
   195→                y={HALF}
   196→                textAnchor="middle"
   197→                dominantBaseline="central"
   198→                fill="#ffffff55"
   199→                fontSize={13}
   200→                fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   201→              >
   202→                {t("pieNoSelection" as TranslationKey)}
   203→              </text>
   204→            </>
   205→          ) : (
   206→            <>
   207→              {/* Segments */}
   208→              {segments.map((seg, i) => {
   209→                const item = items[i];
   210→                const isHovered = hoveredIndex === i;
   211→                const fillColor = isHovered ? `${item.color}30` : "#ffffff05";
   212→                const strokeColor = isHovered ? `${item.color}55` : "#ffffff08";
   213→
   214→                return (
   215→                  <path
   216→                    key={item.id}
   217→                    d={describeDonutSegment(HALF, HALF, RING_INNER, RING_OUTER, seg.start, seg.end)}
   218→                    fill={fillColor}
   219→                    stroke={strokeColor}
   220→                    strokeWidth={1}
   221→                    style={{ transition: "fill 100ms ease, stroke 100ms ease" }}
   222→                  />
   223→                );
   224→              })}
   225→
   226→              {/* Cursor indicator arc */}
   227→              {state.mouseR >= RING_INNER && hoveredIndex >= 0 && (
   228→                <path
   229→                  d={describeDonutSegment(
   230→                    HALF, HALF,
   231→                    RING_OUTER - 2, RING_OUTER + 4,
   232→                    state.mouseAngle - INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG / 2,
   233→                    state.mouseAngle + INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG / 2,
   234→                  )}
   235→                  fill={items[hoveredIndex]?.color ?? "#8b5cf6"}
   236→                  filter="url(#pie-menu-glow)"
   237→                  opacity={0.9}
   238→                />
   239→              )}
   240→
   241→              {/* Labels */}
   242→              {segments.map((seg, i) => {
   243→                const item = items[i];
   244→                const isHovered = hoveredIndex === i;
   245→                const labelX = HALF + LABEL_R * Math.cos(deg2rad(seg.center));
   246→                const labelY = HALF + LABEL_R * Math.sin(deg2rad(seg.center));
   247→
   248→                const displayLabel = t(item.labelKey as TranslationKey);
   249→                const IconComponent = item.icon;
   250→
   251→                const textFill = isHovered ? "#fff" : "#ffffff88";
   252→                const bgFill = "#0e0e1cdd";
   253→                const fs = item.fontSize ?? 11;
   254→                const ICON_SIZE = 14;
   255→                const iconSpace = IconComponent ? ICON_SIZE + 6 : 0;
   256→                const textWidth = iconSpace + displayLabel.length * fs * 0.65 + 20;
   257→                const textHeight = Math.max(fs + 10, ICON_SIZE + 8);
   258→
   259→                return (
   260→                  <g
   261→                    key={item.id}
   262→                    className="pie-menu-label-group"
   263→                    style={{
   264→                      animation: `piePopIn ${POP_IN_MS}ms ease-out ${i * STAGGER_MS}ms both`,
   265→                    }}
   266→                  >
   267→                    {/* Background rect */}
   268→                    <rect
   269→                      x={labelX - textWidth / 2}
   270→                      y={labelY - textHeight / 2}
   271→                      width={textWidth}
   272→                      height={textHeight}
   273→                      rx={4}
   274→                      fill={bgFill}
   275→                      stroke={isHovered ? `${item.color}55` : "transparent"}
   276→                      strokeWidth={1}
   277→                    />
   278→                    {/* Color dot for leaf color items (no icon) */}
   279→                    {!IconComponent && (item.isColor || item.action) ? (
   280→                      <circle
   281→                        cx={labelX - textWidth / 2 + 10}
   282→                        cy={labelY}
   283→                        r={4}
   284→                        fill={item.color}
   285→                      />
   286→                    ) : null}
   287→                    {/* Lucide icon via foreignObject */}
   288→                    {IconComponent && (
   289→                      <foreignObject
   290→                        x={labelX - textWidth / 2 + 6}
   291→                        y={labelY - ICON_SIZE / 2}
   292→                        width={ICON_SIZE}
   293→                        height={ICON_SIZE}
   294→                      >
   295→                        <IconComponent
   296→                          size={ICON_SIZE}
   297→                          style={{ color: isHovered ? "#fff" : item.color, transition: "color 100ms ease" }}
   298→                        />
   299→                      </foreignObject>
   300→                    )}
   301→                    {/* Text label */}
   302→                    <text
   303→                      x={IconComponent ? labelX - textWidth / 2 + 6 + ICON_SIZE + 4 + (textWidth - iconSpace - 20) / 2 : labelX}
   304→                      y={labelY}
   305→                      textAnchor="middle"
   306→                      dominantBaseline="central"
   307→                      fill={textFill}
   308→                      fontSize={fs}
   309→                      fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   310→                      style={{
   311→                        ...item.labelStyle,
   312→                        transition: "fill 100ms ease",
   313→                      }}
   314→                    >
   315→                      {displayLabel}
   316→                    </text>
   317→                  </g>
   318→                );
   319→              })}
   320→
   321→              {/* Center deadzone */}
   322→              <circle
   323→                cx={HALF}
   324→                cy={HALF}
   325→                r={DEADZONE}
   326→                fill="#0e0e1ccc"
   327→                stroke="#ffffff15"
   328→                strokeWidth={1}
   329→              />
   330→              {/* Breadcrumb text */}
   331→              {breadcrumb ? (
   332→                <text
   333→                  x={HALF}
   334→                  y={HALF}
   335→                  textAnchor="middle"
   336→                  dominantBaseline="central"
   337→                  fill="#ffffffaa"
   338→                  fontSize={9}
   339→                  fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   340→                >
   341→                  {breadcrumb.length > 12 ? "‹" : breadcrumb || "‹"}
   342→                </text>
   343→              ) : (
   344→                <text
   345→                  x={HALF}
   346→                  y={HALF}
   347→                  textAnchor="middle"
   348→                  dominantBaseline="central"
   349→                  fill="#ffffff55"
   350→                  fontSize={10}
   351→                  fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   352→                >
   353→                  ●
   354→                </text>
   355→              )}
   356→            </>
   357→          )}
   358→        </svg>
   359→      </div>
   360→    </div>
   361→  );
   362→}
   363→
```

> AGENT

方針:
- **リング**: SVGの後ろにHTML divでドーナツ型のblurレイヤーを配置（`clip-path`で切り抜き）
- **ラベル**: SVG `<rect>` + `<text>` → `foreignObject` 内のHTML divに変更（`backdrop-filter: blur`）
- **中心**: SVG circle → `foreignObject` 内のHTML divに変更

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport React, { useMemo } from \"react\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport type { TranslationKey } from \"@/lib/i18n/locales\";\nimport {\n  type PieMenuState,\n  DEADZONE,\n  RING_INNER,\n  RING_OUTER,\n  LABEL_R,\n  GAP_DEG,\n  FADE_MS,\n  getSegmentAngles,\n} from \"@/hooks/usePieMenu\";\nimport type { PieMenuItem } from \"@/lib/pieMenuData\";\n\n// ── Constants ───────────────────────────────────────────────\nconst CLOSE_R = LABEL_R + 60;\nconst INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG = 6;\nconst STAGGER_MS = 18;\nconst POP_IN_MS = 140;\nconst SVG_SIZE = (CLOSE_R + 20) * 2;\nconst HALF = SVG_SIZE / 2;\nconst BLUR_AMOUNT = 12;\n\n// ── Glassmorphism shared styles ─────────────────────────────\nconst GLASS_BG = \"rgba(14, 14, 28, 0.45)\";\nconst GLASS_BG_HOVER = \"rgba(14, 14, 28, 0.55)\";\nconst GLASS_BORDER = \"rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)\";\nconst GLASS_BORDER_HOVER_ALPHA = \"55\"; // appended to item.color\n\n// ── Geometry helpers ────────────────────────────────────────\nconst deg2rad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;\n\nfunction describeDonutSegment(\n  cx: number, cy: number,\n  innerR: number, outerR: number,\n  startDeg: number, endDeg: number,\n): string {\n  const s1 = deg2rad(startDeg);\n  const e1 = deg2rad(endDeg);\n  const sweep = endDeg - startDeg;\n  const large = sweep > 180 ? 1 : 0;\n\n  const ox1 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(s1);\n  const oy1 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(s1);\n  const ox2 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(e1);\n  const oy2 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(e1);\n  const ix1 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(e1);\n  const iy1 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(e1);\n  const ix2 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(s1);\n  const iy2 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(s1);\n\n  return [\n    `M ${ox1} ${oy1}`,\n    `A ${outerR} ${outerR} 0 ${large} 1 ${ox2} ${oy2}`,\n    `L ${ix1} ${iy1}`,\n    `A ${innerR} ${innerR} 0 ${large} 0 ${ix2} ${iy2}`,\n    \"Z\",\n  ].join(\" \");\n}\n\nfunction hitTest(angle: number, r: number, items: PieMenuItem[], angleOffset: number): number {\n  if (r < DEADZONE) return -2; // deadzone\n  if (r > CLOSE_R) return -3;  // far away\n  if (items.length === 0) return -1;\n\n  const angles = getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset);\n  for (let i = 0; i < angles.length; i++) {\n    let { start, end } = angles[i];\n    start = ((start % 360) + 360) % 360;\n    end = ((end % 360) + 360) % 360;\n\n    let inRange: boolean;\n    if (start <= end) {\n      inRange = angle >= start && angle <= end;\n    } else {\n      inRange = angle >= start || angle <= end;\n    }\n    if (inRange && r >= RING_INNER - 10 && r <= CLOSE_R) return i;\n  }\n  return -1;\n}\n\n// ── Props ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\ninterface PieMenuProps {\n  state: PieMenuState;\n  items: PieMenuItem[];\n  angleOffset: number;\n  rootMenu: PieMenuItem[] | null;\n  breadcrumb: string;\n  onDrillDown: (index: number) => void;\n  onDrillUp: () => void;\n  onDispatch: (action: NonNullable<PieMenuItem[\"action\"]>) => void;\n  onClose: () => void;\n}\n\n// ── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────\nexport default function PieMenu({\n  state,\n  items,\n  angleOffset,\n  rootMenu,\n  breadcrumb,\n  onDrillDown,\n  onDrillUp,\n  onDispatch,\n  onClose,\n}: PieMenuProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  const segments = useMemo(\n    () => (items.length > 0 ? getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset) : []),\n    [items, angleOffset],\n  );\n\n  const hoveredIndex = items.length > 0\n    ? hitTest(state.mouseAngle, state.mouseR, items, angleOffset)\n    : -1;\n\n  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    e.preventDefault();\n\n    if (hoveredIndex === -2) {\n      onDrillUp();\n      return;\n    }\n    if (hoveredIndex === -3 || hoveredIndex === -1) {\n      onClose();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const item = items[hoveredIndex];\n    if (!item) return;\n\n    if (item.children && item.children.length > 0) {\n      onDrillDown(hoveredIndex);\n    } else if (item.action) {\n      onDispatch(item.action);\n      onClose();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const noSelection = !rootMenu;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        position: \"fixed\",\n        inset: 0,\n        zIndex: 2000,\n        pointerEvents: \"none\",\n      }}\n    >\n      <div\n        style={{\n          position: \"absolute\",\n          left: state.position.x - HALF,\n          top: state.position.y - HALF,\n          width: SVG_SIZE,\n          height: SVG_SIZE,\n          pointerEvents: \"auto\",\n          cursor: \"default\",\n          opacity: state.isFading ? 0 : 1,\n          transition: `opacity ${FADE_MS}ms ease`,\n        }}\n        onClick={handleClick}\n        onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); }}\n      >\n        {/* ── Glass layer 1: Ring blur backdrop ── */}\n        <div\n          style={{\n            position: \"absolute\",\n            left: HALF - RING_OUTER - 2,\n            top: HALF - RING_OUTER - 2,\n            width: (RING_OUTER + 2) * 2,\n            height: (RING_OUTER + 2) * 2,\n            borderRadius: \"50%\",\n            backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT}px)`,\n            WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT}px)`,\n            background: GLASS_BG,\n            border: `1px solid ${GLASS_BORDER}`,\n            // Cut out the inner circle to make a donut\n            mask: `radial-gradient(circle at center, transparent ${RING_INNER - 1}px, black ${RING_INNER}px)`,\n            WebkitMask: `radial-gradient(circle at center, transparent ${RING_INNER - 1}px, black ${RING_INNER}px)`,\n          }}\n        />\n\n        {/* ── Glass layer 2: Center deadzone blur backdrop ── */}\n        {!noSelection && (\n          <div\n            style={{\n              position: \"absolute\",\n              left: HALF - DEADZONE,\n              top: HALF - DEADZONE,\n              width: DEADZONE * 2,\n              height: DEADZONE * 2,\n              borderRadius: \"50%\",\n              backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT + 4}px)`,\n              WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT + 4}px)`,\n              background: \"rgba(14, 14, 28, 0.55)\",\n              border: `1px solid ${GLASS_BORDER}`,\n              display: \"flex\",\n              alignItems: \"center\",\n              justifyContent: \"center\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {breadcrumb ? (\n              <span style={{ color: \"#ffffffaa\", fontSize: 9, fontFamily: \"system-ui, sans-serif\", userSelect: \"none\" }}>\n                {breadcrumb.length > 12 ? \"‹\" : breadcrumb || \"‹\"}\n              </span>\n            ) : (\n              <span style={{ color: \"#ffffff55\", fontSize: 10, fontFamily: \"system-ui, sans-serif\", userSelect: \"none\" }}>●</span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* ── SVG overlay: segments, indicators ── */}\n        <svg\n          width={SVG_SIZE}\n          height={SVG_SIZE}\n          viewBox={`0 0 ${SVG_SIZE} ${SVG_SIZE}`}\n          style={{ position: \"absolute\", inset: 0, overflow: \"visible\", pointerEvents: \"none\" }}\n        >\n          <defs>\n            <filter id=\"pie-menu-glow\" x=\"-50%\" y=\"-50%\" width=\"200%\" height=\"200%\">\n              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation=\"4\" result=\"blur\" />\n              <feMerge>\n                <feMergeNode in=\"blur\" />\n                <feMergeNode in=\"SourceGraphic\" />\n              </feMerge>\n            </filter>\n          </defs>\n\n          {noSelection ? (\n            <>\n              <circle cx={HALF} cy={HALF} r={RING_OUTER} fill=\"transparent\" stroke={GLASS_BORDER} strokeWidth={1} />\n              <text\n                x={HALF} y={HALF}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\" dominantBaseline=\"central\"\n                fill=\"#ffffff55\" fontSize={13} fontFamily=\"system-ui, sans-serif\"\n              >\n                {t(\"pieNoSelection\" as TranslationKey)}\n              </text>\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <>\n              {/* Segment outlines (on top of glass backdrop) */}\n              {segments.map((seg, i) => {\n                const item = items[i];\n                const isHovered = hoveredIndex === i;\n                const fillColor = isHovered ? `${item.color}25` : \"transparent\";\n                const strokeColor = isHovered ? `${item.color}${GLASS_BORDER_HOVER_ALPHA}` : GLASS_BORDER;\n\n                return (\n                  <path\n                    key={item.id}\n                    d={describeDonutSegment(HALF, HALF, RING_INNER, RING_OUTER, seg.start, seg.end)}\n                    fill={fillColor}\n                    stroke={strokeColor}\n                    strokeWidth={1}\n                    style={{ transition: \"fill 100ms ease, stroke 100ms ease\", pointerEvents: \"auto\" }}\n                  />\n                );\n              })}\n\n              {/* Cursor indicator arc */}\n              {state.mouseR >= RING_INNER && hoveredIndex >= 0 && (\n                <path\n                  d={describeDonutSegment(\n                    HALF, HALF,\n                    RING_OUTER - 2, RING_OUTER + 4,\n                    state.mouseAngle - INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG / 2,\n                    state.mouseAngle + INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG / 2,\n                  )}\n                  fill={items[hoveredIndex]?.color ?? \"#8b5cf6\"}\n                  filter=\"url(#pie-menu-glow)\"\n                  opacity={0.9}\n                />\n              )}\n            </>\n          )}\n        </svg>\n\n        {/* ── Glass layer 3: Labels (HTML for backdrop-filter) ── */}\n        {!noSelection && segments.map((seg, i) => {\n          const item = items[i];\n          const isHovered = hoveredIndex === i;\n          const labelX = HALF + LABEL_R * Math.cos(deg2rad(seg.center));\n          const labelY = HALF + LABEL_R * Math.sin(deg2rad(seg.center));\n\n          const displayLabel = t(item.labelKey as TranslationKey);\n          const IconComponent = item.icon;\n          const fs = item.fontSize ?? 11;\n          const ICON_SIZE = 14;\n\n          return (\n            <div\n              key={item.id}\n              className=\"pie-menu-label-group\"\n              style={{\n                position: \"absolute\",\n                left: labelX,\n                top: labelY,\n                transform: \"translate(-50%, -50%)\",\n                animation: `piePopIn ${POP_IN_MS}ms ease-out ${i * STAGGER_MS}ms both`,\n                pointerEvents: \"none\",\n              }}\n            >\n              <div\n                style={{\n                  display: \"inline-flex\",\n                  alignItems: \"center\",\n                  gap: 5,\n                  padding: \"4px 10px\",\n                  borderRadius: 6,\n                  backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT}px)`,\n                  WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_AMOUNT}px)`,\n                  background: isHovered ? **変更内容:**

1. **セグメントリング** — SVGの背後にHTML divでドーナツ型の`backdrop-filter: blur(12px)` + 半透明背景を配置。`radial-gradient` maskで内側を切り抜いてドーナツ形状を実現。SVGセグメントは塗りを透過に変更し、ホバー時のみアクセントカラーが浮かぶ。

2. **ラベル** — SVGの`<rect>`+`<text>`からHTML divに完全移行。`backdrop-filter: blur(12px)` + `rgba(14,14,28,0.45)` 背景 + `rgba(255,255,255,0.08)` ボーダー。ホバー時は背景を少し濃くし、アクセントカラーのボーダー + `box-shadow` グローを追加。

3. **中心デッドゾーン** — SVG circleからHTML divに変更。`backdrop-filter: blur(16px)` + 円形のすりガラス。パンくずテキストはそのまま内部に配置。

> AGENT

問題を特定しました:

1. **半透明にならない**: `mask` を使うと `backdrop-filter` が無効化される（ブラウザの制限）。リングのドーナツ形状はmaskではなく別の方法が必要。
2. **ラベル位置ずれ**: コンテナに `position: relative` がなく、子要素の `position: absolute` の基準がずれている。

修正します。

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport React, { useMemo } from \"react\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport type { TranslationKey } from \"@/lib/i18n/locales\";\nimport {\n  type PieMenuState,\n  DEADZONE,\n  RING_INNER,\n  RING_OUTER,\n  LABEL_R,\n  GAP_DEG,\n  FADE_MS,\n  getSegmentAngles,\n} from \"@/hooks/usePieMenu\";\nimport type { PieMenuItem } from \"@/lib/pieMenuData\";\n\n// ── Constants ───────────────────────────────────────────────\nconst CLOSE_R = LABEL_R + 60;\nconst INDICATOR_WIDTH_DEG = 6;\nconst STAGGER_MS = 18;\nconst POP_IN_MS = 140;\nconst SVG_SIZE = (CLOSE_R + 20) * 2;\nconst HALF = SVG_SIZE / 2;\nconst BLUR_PX = 16;\n\n// ── Glassmorphism tokens ────────────────────────────────────\nconst GLASS_BG = \"rgba(20, 20, 36, 0.35)\";\nconst GLASS_BG_HOVER = \"rgba(20, 20, 36, 0.5)\";\nconst GLASS_BORDER = \"rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)\";\n\n// ── Geometry helpers ────────────────────────────────────────\nconst deg2rad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180;\n\nfunction describeDonutSegment(\n  cx: number, cy: number,\n  innerR: number, outerR: number,\n  startDeg: number, endDeg: number,\n): string {\n  const s1 = deg2rad(startDeg);\n  const e1 = deg2rad(endDeg);\n  const sweep = endDeg - startDeg;\n  const large = sweep > 180 ? 1 : 0;\n\n  const ox1 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(s1);\n  const oy1 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(s1);\n  const ox2 = cx + outerR * Math.cos(e1);\n  const oy2 = cy + outerR * Math.sin(e1);\n  const ix1 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(e1);\n  const iy1 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(e1);\n  const ix2 = cx + innerR * Math.cos(s1);\n  const iy2 = cy + innerR * Math.sin(s1);\n\n  return [\n    `M ${ox1} ${oy1}`,\n    `A ${outerR} ${outerR} 0 ${large} 1 ${ox2} ${oy2}`,\n    `L ${ix1} ${iy1}`,\n    `A ${innerR} ${innerR} 0 ${large} 0 ${ix2} ${iy2}`,\n    \"Z\",\n  ].join(\" \");\n}\n\nfunction hitTest(angle: number, r: number, items: PieMenuItem[], angleOffset: number): number {\n  if (r < DEADZONE) return -2;\n  if (r > CLOSE_R) return -3;\n  if (items.length === 0) return -1;\n\n  const angles = getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset);\n  for (let i = 0; i < angles.length; i++) {\n    let { start, end } = angles[i];\n    start = ((start % 360) + 360) % 360;\n    end = ((end % 360) + 360) % 360;\n    let inRange: boolean;\n    if (start <= end) {\n      inRange = angle >= start && angle <= end;\n    } else {\n      inRange = angle >= start || angle <= end;\n    }\n    if (inRange && r >= RING_INNER - 10 && r <= CLOSE_R) return i;\n  }\n  return -1;\n}\n\n// ── Props ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\ninterface PieMenuProps {\n  state: PieMenuState;\n  items: PieMenuItem[];\n  angleOffset: number;\n  rootMenu: PieMenuItem[] | null;\n  breadcrumb: string;\n  onDrillDown: (index: number) => void;\n  onDrillUp: () => void;\n  onDispatch: (action: NonNullable<PieMenuItem[\"action\"]>) => void;\n  onClose: () => void;\n}\n\n// ── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────\nexport default function PieMenu({\n  state,\n  items,\n  angleOffset,\n  rootMenu,\n  breadcrumb,\n  onDrillDown,\n  onDrillUp,\n  onDispatch,\n  onClose,\n}: PieMenuProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  const segments = useMemo(\n    () => (items.length > 0 ? getSegmentAngles(items.length, angleOffset) : []),\n    [items, angleOffset],\n  );\n\n  const hoveredIndex = items.length > 0\n    ? hitTest(state.mouseAngle, state.mouseR, items, angleOffset)\n    : -1;\n\n  const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (hoveredIndex === -2) { onDrillUp(); return; }\n    if (hoveredIndex === -3 || hoveredIndex === -1) { onClose(); return; }\n    const item = items[hoveredIndex];\n    if (!item) return;\n    if (item.children && item.children.length > 0) {\n      onDrillDown(hoveredIndex);\n    } else if (item.action) {\n      onDispatch(item.action);\n      onClose();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const noSelection = !rootMenu;\n  const ICON_SIZE = 14;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{ position: \"fixed\", inset: 0, zIndex: 2000, pointerEvents: \"none\" }}\n    >\n      {/* ── Container (position context for all children) ── */}\n      <div\n        style={{\n          position: \"absolute\",\n          left: state.position.x - HALF,\n          top: state.position.y - HALF,\n          width: SVG_SIZE,\n          height: SVG_SIZE,\n          pointerEvents: \"auto\",\n          cursor: \"default\",\n          opacity: state.isFading ? 0 : 1,\n          transition: `opacity ${FADE_MS}ms ease`,\n        }}\n        onClick={handleClick}\n        onContextMenu={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); }}\n      >\n        {/* ── Glass ring: outer circle (blur) ── */}\n        <div\n          style={{\n            position: \"absolute\",\n            left: HALF - RING_OUTER - 2,\n            top: HALF - RING_OUTER - 2,\n            width: (RING_OUTER + 2) * 2,\n            height: (RING_OUTER + 2) * 2,\n            borderRadius: \"50%\",\n            backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX}px)`,\n            WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX}px)`,\n            background: GLASS_BG,\n            border: `1px solid ${GLASS_BORDER}`,\n            pointerEvents: \"none\",\n          }}\n        />\n        {/* ── Glass ring: inner hole (punch out) ── */}\n        <div\n          style={{\n            position: \"absolute\",\n            left: HALF - DEADZONE,\n              top: HALF - DEADZONE,\n              width: DEADZONE * 2,\n              height: DEADZONE * 2,\n              borderRadius: \"50%\",\n              backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX + 4}px)`,\n              WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX + 4}px)`,\n              background: \"rgba(20, 20, 36, 0.45)\",\n              border: `1px solid ${GLASS_BORDER}`,\n              display: \"flex\",\n              alignItems: \"center\",\n              justifyContent: \"center\",\n              zIndex: 2,\n              pointerEvents: \"none\",\n            }}\n          >\n            {breadcrumb ? (\n              <span style={{ color: \"#ffffffaa\", fontSize: 9, fontFamily: \"system-ui, sans-serif\", userSelect: \"none\" }}>\n                {breadcrumb.length > 12 ? \"‹\" : breadcrumb || \"‹\"}\n              </span>\n            ) : (\n              <span style={{ color: \"#ffffff55\", fontSize: 10, fontFamily: \"system-ui, sans-serif\", userSelect: \"none\" }}>●</span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* ── SVG layer: segment fills, outlines, indicator ── */}\n        <svg\n          width={SVG_SIZE}\n          height={SVG_SIZE}\n          viewBox={`0 0 ${SVG_SIZE} ${SVG_SIZE}`}\n          style={{ position: \"absolute\", left: 0, top: 0, overflow: \"visible\", pointerEvents: \"none\" }}\n        >\n          <defs>\n            <filter id=\"pie-menu-glow\" x=\"-50%\" y=\"-50%\" width=\"200%\" height=\"200%\">\n              <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation=\"4\" result=\"blur\" />\n              <feMerge>\n                <feMergeNode in=\"blur\" />\n                <feMergeNode in=\"SourceGraphic\" />\n              </feMerge>\n            </filter>\n          </defs>\n\n          {noSelection ? (\n            <>\n              <circle cx={HALF} cy={HALF} r={RING_OUTER} fill=\"transparent\" stroke={GLASS_BORDER} strokeWidth={1} />\n              <text\n                x={HALF} y={HALF}\n                textAnchor=\"middle\" dominantBaseline=\"central\"\n                fill=\"#ffffff55\" fontSize={13} fontFamily=\"system-ui, sans-serif\"\n              >\n                {t(\"pieNoSelection\" as TranslationKey)}\n              </text>\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <>\n              {/* Segment hover fills + dividers */}\n              {segments.map((seg, i) => {\n                const item = items[i];\n                const isHovered = hoveredIndex === i;\n                return (\n                  <path\n                    key={item.id}\n                    d={describeDonutSegment(HALF, HALF, RING_INNER, RING_OUTER, seg.start, seg.end)}\n                    fill={isHovered ? 11;\n\n          return (\n            <div\n              key={item.id}\n              className=\"pie-menu-label-group\"\n              style={{\n                position: \"absolute\",\n                left: cx,\n                top: cy,\n                transform: \"translate(-50%, -50%)\",\n                animation: `piePopIn ${POP_IN_MS}ms ease-out ${i * STAGGER_MS}ms both`,\n                pointerEvents: \"none\",\n                zIndex: 3,\n              }}\n            >\n              <div\n                style={{\n                  display: \"inline-flex\",\n                  alignItems: \"center\",\n                  gap: 5,\n                  padding: \"4px 10px\",\n                  borderRadius: 6,\n                  backdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX}px)`,\n                  WebkitBackdropFilter: `blur(${BLUR_PX}px)`,\n                  background: isHovered ? > AGENT

型エラーなし。修正内容:

1. **半透明問題**: `mask`/`-webkit-mask` を削除。`mask` と `backdrop-filter` は併用不可（ブラウザ制限でblurが無効化される）。リングは単純な円形divの `backdrop-filter: blur(16px)` + `rgba(20,20,36,0.35)` で透過するようにした。

2. **ラベル位置ずれ**: ラベルの `left`/`top` を SVG座標系と一致する `cx`/`cy` に修正し、`transform: translate(-50%, -50%)` で中心合わせ。`zIndex: 3` で SVG層の上に確実に描画。

3. **内側の穴**: maskの代わりに内側円を別div（`isolation: isolate` + 透明背景）で重��る方式に変更。中心デッドゾーンは独立した `backdrop-filter: blur(20px)` で別途すりガラス化。

> DEVELOPER

変わっていないよ。
他のところに、原因なんじゃないん？

> AGENT

`backdrop-filter` が効かない原因を調べます。親要素のCSS（`transform`, `will-change`, `filter` 等）が邪魔している可能性が高い。

> AGENT

原因がわかりました。PieMenuは `FlowCanvas` の中（`<div className="w-full h-full">` の内部）にマウントされています。これは `ResizablePanel` → `overflow-hidden` の中です。

`position: fixed` を使っても、**親に `will-change` や CSS `transform` を持つ要素があると `backdrop-filter` の基準がずれる** — そしてReact Flowが内部で `transform` を多用しています。

**解決策**: PieMenuをFlowCanvas内ではなく、React Portalで `document.body` に直接マウントする。

> AGENT

全パス。

**原因**: PieMenuは `FlowCanvas` → `ResizablePanel`（`overflow: hidden`）→ `ReactFlow`（内部で `transform` を多用）の中にマウントされていた。祖���に `transform` があると `backdrop-filter` の描画基準がその要素になり、背後のキャンバスをblurできなくなる。

**修正**: `createPortal(... , document.body)` で `document.body` に直接マウント。これで祖先のCSS影響を完全に回避し、`backdrop-filter: blur()` がキャンバス背景に対して正しく効くよ���になった。

> AGENT

スクリーンショットをよく見ると、ラベルはセグメントの方向には合っていますが、**リングの中心に対してずれている**。つまりリングとラベルの中心が一致していない。

原因がわかりました。コンテナdivの `left`/`top` は `state.position.x - HALF` で設定していますが、HTML要素の `left`/`top` はCSSのpx値で、`border` の1pxやリングdivの位置計算で微妙にずれています。

実はもっと根本的な問題がありそうです。SVGの座標系（`viewBox` ベース）とHTML divの `position: absolute`（CSS px ベース）が、SVGの `width`/`height` と `viewBox` が同一スケール（1:1）なので理論的には一致するはずですが、**SVG自体に `left: 0, top: 0` を設定しているのに対し、HTML divのラベルは親コンテナ基準で配置している**。

問題を正確に見つけました。SVGの座標（`HALF`中心）とHTMLラベルの座標は一致しているはず。スクリーンショートを見るとラベルは**セグメントの外周の延長線上に正しく配置されている**。ずれているのは**セグメントの方**です。

画像を改めて見ると：「カラー」が右、「テキスト」が上、「順序」が下、「枠線」が左上、「整列」が左下 — これは5セグメントで0°開始の配置として正しい。ラベルもその方向にある。

ただし、ラベルがリングから**離れすぎている**のが問題のように見えます。`LABEL_R = 128` で `RING_OUTER = 88` なので、40pxのギャップがある。これが「ずれ」に見えている可能性があります。

`LABEL_R` を小さくしてリングに近づけましょう。

> AGENT

問題がわかりました。外側の div に `transform: translate(-50%, -50%)` をかけていますが、この div は `position: absolute` で中身のサイズに依存しません。一方、内側の div は `display: "inline-flex"` で内容に応じた幅になります。

この構造だと、外側 div のサイズが 0×0 になり、`translate(-50%, -50%)` は何もしない（0の50%は0）。結果、ラベルの**左上隅**が `(cx, cy)` に配置されてしまいます。

> AGENT

この2個目の「inner hole」divは、外側リングのblurを内側で打ち消す意図で置いていますが、`backdrop-filter: blur(0px)` + `isolation: isolate` では実際には打ち消せません。不要なので削除します。

代わりに、外側リング自体を `mask` なしのドーナツにするために、1つ目のdivに `clip-path` で穴を開けます。`clip-path` は `backdrop-filter` を阻害しません。

> AGENT

セグメントの外周弧部分だけ消すには、セグメントの `stroke` を内側の区切り線だけに変える必要があります。セグメント間のギャップ線（放射状の区切り）は `stroke` で描かれていますが、外周・内周の弧も同じ `stroke` です。

SVGの `path` ではストロークを部分的に消せないので、セグメントの stroke を消して、区切り線だけ別の `line` で描きます。

> AGENT

問題がわかりました。`backdrop-filter` は内側の `div` にかかっていますが、外側のラッパー div に `zIndex: 3` があり、内側 div に独自のスタッキングコンテキストがありません。`backdrop-filter` が効くにはその要素自体がスタッキングコンテキストを形成する必要がありますが、`inline-flex` の div には `position` が設定されていません。

ラベルがリングの外側にある部分では効くはずです。`LABEL_R=110` で `RING_OUTER=88` なので、ラベルはリングの外に出ているはず…ですが、オフセット(`-16`, `-8`)でリングに被っている可能性があります。

> AGENT

問題の全体像を把握しました。ラベルの構造:

1. 固定オーバーレイ div (`position: fixed, inset: 0`) 
2. → コンテナ div (`position: absolute`, `opacity` transition付き)
3. → → ラベルラッパー div (`position: absolute`, `animation` 付き, `zIndex: 3`)
4. → → → ラベル内容 div (`position: relative`, `backdrop-filter`, `isolation`)

`backdrop-filter` が効かない原因の候補:
- 親の `opacity` transition（`opacity` を変更するとレイヤーが作られる）
- 親の `animation`（既にtransform削除済み）
- `zIndex` がスタッキングコンテキストを作る
- 親コンテナの `position: absolute` + `opacity` の組み合わせ

`opacity` がトランジション対象になっているコンテナが問題です。

> TOOL

解決策: `transform` を使わずにセンタリングする。

> TOOL
