    68→      }
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    // Check base swatches
    72→    for (let ci = 0; ci < items.length; ci++) {
    73→      const pos = baseSwatchPos(ci, items.length);
    74→      const r = selectedBase !== null && ci !== selectedBase ? 6 : SWATCH_R;
    75→      if (hitTestSwatch(mouseX, mouseY, pos.x, pos.y, r)) {
    76→        return { type: "base" as const, ci };
    77→      }
    78→    }
    79→
    80→    // Check deadzone
    81→    if (mouseX * mouseX + mouseY * mouseY <= COLOR_DEADZONE * COLOR_DEADZONE) {
    82→      return { type: "center" as const };
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    // Check far away
    86→    const dist = Math.sqrt(mouseX * mouseX + mouseY * mouseY);
    87→    if (dist > BASE_RING_R + PETAL_DIST + SHADE_R + 30) {
    88→      return { type: "far" as const };
    89→    }
    90→
    91→    return null;
    92→  }, [mouseX, mouseY, selectedBase, items.length]);
    93→
    94→  // Preview on hover
    95→  React.useEffect(() => {
    96→    if (hovered?.type === "petal" && selectedBase !== null) {
    97→      const variant = variants[hovered.ci][hovered.li];
    98→      const baseItem = items[hovered.ci];
    99→      if (baseItem?.action) {
   100→        // Create a modified action with the variant color
   101→        const action = { ...baseItem.action, color: variant.css } as PieMenuAction;
   102→        onPreview(action);
   103→      }
   104→    } else if (hovered?.type === "base") {
   105→      const item = items[hovered.ci];
   106→      if (item?.action) onPreview(item.action);
   107→    } else {
   108→      onPreview(undefined);
   109→    }
   110→  }, [hovered, selectedBase, variants, items, onPreview]);

   112→  const handleClick = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent) => {

   114→    e.preventDefault();
   115→
   116→    if (!hovered) return;
   117→
   118→    switch (hovered.type) {
   119→      case "base": {
   120→        if (selectedBase === hovered.ci) {
   121→          // Same base clicked → go back to base phase
   122→          setSelectedBase(null);
   123→        } else {
   124→          // Select or switch base
   125→          setSelectedBase(hovered.ci);
   126→        }
   127→        break;
   128→      }
   129→      case "petal": {
   130→        // Confirm color
   131→        const variant = variants[hovered.ci][hovered.li];
   132→        const baseItem = items[hovered.ci];
   133→        if (baseItem?.action) {
   134→          const action = { ...baseItem.action, color: variant.css } as PieMenuAction;
   135→          onSelectColor(action);
   136→        }
   137→        break;
   138→      }
   139→      case "center": {
   140→        if (selectedBase !== null) {
   141→          setSelectedBase(null);
   142→        } else {
   143→          onBack();
   144→        }
   145→        break;
   146→      }
   147→      case "far":
   148→        onClose();
   149→        break;
   150→    }
   151→  }, [hovered, selectedBase, variants, items, onSelectColor, onBack, onClose]);

   153→  const isShadePhase = selectedBase !== null;

   155→  return (
   156→    <div
   157→      style={{
   158→        width: CONTAINER_SIZE,
   159→        height: CONTAINER_SIZE,
   160→        position: "relative",

   162→        transition: `opacity ${fadeDuration}ms ease`,

   166→    >

   172→      >

   174→        {isShadePhase && Array.from({ length: LIGHTNESS_STEPS }, (_, li) => {

   176→          const petal = petalPos(selectedBase, li, items.length);

   183→              y2={CENTER + petal.y}

   225→                cursor: "pointer",
   226→                pointerEvents: "auto",
   227→              }}
   228→            />
   229→          </div>

   231→      })}
   232→
   233→      {/* Petal swatches (shade phase only) */}
   234→      {isShadePhase && variants[selectedBase].map((variant, li) => {
   235→        const pos = petalPos(selectedBase, li, items.length);
   236→        const isHovered = hovered?.type === "petal" && hovered.ci === selectedBase && hovered.li === li;
   237→        const lightnessLabel = `${Math.round(20 + li * 20)}%`;
   238→
   239→        return (

   291→      {/* Center disc */}

   298→          height: COLOR_DEADZONE * 2,

   300→          background: hovered?.type === "petal" && selectedBase !== null

   302→            : hovered?.type === "base"
   303→              ? items[hovered.ci]?.color ?? "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)"
   304→              : "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",

   316→      >

   319→        )}

   321→    </div>

RING_BG\n              : RING_BG,\n          border: `1px solid ${GLASS_BORDER}`,\n          boxShadow: SHADOW,\n          display: \"flex\",\n          alignItems: \"center\",\n          justifyContent: \"center\",\n          transition: `background ${TRANSITION}`,\n          cursor: \"pointer\",\n          pointerEvents: \"auto\",\n          zIndex: 20,\n        }}\n      >\n        {isShadePhase && (\n          <span style={{ color: \"#ffffffaa\", fontSize: 12, userSelect: \"none\" }}>‹</span>\n        )}\n        {!isShadePhase && (\n          <span style={{ color: \"#ffffff55\", fontSize: 10, userSelect: \"none\" }}>●</span>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

PetalColorPickerをパイメニューと統一デザインに書き直しました:

- **スウォッチ**: 丸い色ドットだけ → **ピル型ラベル**（カラードット + テキスト）に変更。`backdropFilter: blur(10px)` + `rgba(0,0,0,0.35)` のグラスモーフィズム
- **ボーダー**: `rgba(255,255,255,0.13)` — パイメニューと同じ
- **影**: `0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)` — パイメニューと同じ
- **ホバー**: `scale(1.05)` + 背景 `rgba(128,128,128,0.3)` + カラーグロー — パイメニューと同じ
- **角丸**: `9999px`（ピル型） — パイメニューと同じ
- **背景リング**: 円形のグラスモーフィズム背景 — パイメニューのドーナツリングと同じ
- **中心**: グラス背景 + ホバー色プレビュー + ‹/● — パイメニューと同じ
- **花びら（shade時）**: 同じピル型ラベル + フェードインアニメーション
